# Function reference

Generated by `npm run docs`. Every first-party function and callback is listed, including tests and tooling. Vendored libraries are documented by their upstream projects; see THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md.

Human-written purpose descriptions explain named helpers. Calls, writes, parameters, and return expressions are derived directly from the source and exclude nested callbacks. A listed return expression is code, not a guarantee about its runtime type. “Writes” lists assignments and updates, not all effects inside called APIs. Read the linked function for full control flow.

## dist/app.js

Base notebook state, legacy migration helpers, navigation, and event wiring. features.js replaces the original storage and drawing handlers after IndexedDB is ready.
